## `AGO-3812-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** TCLP-Metals Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Hazardous Waste Compatibility Screening & Corrosivity Lab
- **Intro:** TCLP-Metals Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Hazardous Waste Compatibility Screening & Corrosivity Lab — Pre-Start Calibration Status Verification: Calibration validity: pH meter ≤ 30 days, balance ≤ 90 days, extractor ≤ 180 days from last calibration date.

- **Standards:** `ISO 17025:2017`, `ISO 5667-3:2012`, `Method 1311`, `Method 9045`
